# Inventory of features from the development tests

Every check of every development test program on 2026-11-22: 6,879 checks in 82 programs. Each line is the sentence a program prints when the check passes; together they say what the software does, every search and filter included, and that it works. Each feature of [features.md](features.md) names the checks that prove it. Generated by `dev-tests/docs/inventory.py` from a complete run ([ADR 0016](adr/0016-documentation-and-feature-inventory.md)); do not edit by hand.

| Area | Programs | Checks |
|---|---|---:|
| [Resolution: the resolver, its pages and its log](#resolution-the-resolver-its-pages-and-its-log) | `resolver`, `gs1_suite`, `epc_binary`, `data_entry_api`, `mock_parity`, `request_log`, `domains_resolver`, `forwarding_resolver`, `strict_resolver`, `language_resolver`, `landing`, `load` | 1,479 |
| [Records: keys, qualifiers, links and the editor](#records-keys-qualifiers-links-and-the-editor) | `keys`, `registration`, `data_attributes`, `special_chars`, `dictionary`, `engine_messages`, `link_types`, `governance`, `targets`, `malformed`, `fix_serials`, `record_delete`, `journal`, `portal_e2e`, `portal_e2e_without_engine`, `link_types_e2e` | 1,437 |

## Every search and filter

The checks of every program about searching, filtering and finding — the record list's text search and filters, a code or a scanner's data, an RFID reader's EPC, the requests', audit trail's and alerts' filters, the API's — gathered from the areas below (they are listed there too).

<details><summary><code>resolver</code> — 3 checks</summary>

- batch A/B (%2F) finds its record
- …a serialised key: 503 after the first read, no search for a shorter stored prefix
- …the database failing during the search for a serialised key's prefix: 503, not 404 nor a shorter prefix

</details>

<details><summary><code>registration</code> — 2 checks</summary>

- opening the serial without its batch finds the record and its informative qualifiers
- opening it with another batch finds the same record

</details>

<details><summary><code>link_types</code> — 1 checks</summary>

- the titles the portal gave before still read as its own, and the vocabulary's too

</details>

<details><summary><code>governance</code> — 1 checks</summary>

- audit trail filtered by user, without record contents

</details>

<details><summary><code>malformed</code> — 6 checks</summary>

- a query operator where a value goes never reaches a database filter: /portal/api/import/apply token
- a query operator where a value goes never reaches a database filter: /portal/api/import/cancel token
- a query operator where a value goes never reaches a database filter: /portal/api/password/link token
- a query operator where a value goes never reaches a database filter: /portal/api/login username
- a query operator where a value goes never reaches a database filter: /portal/api/password/forgot login
- a query operator where a value goes never reaches a database filter: /portal/api/support tenant

</details>

<details><summary><code>portal_e2e</code> — 25 checks</summary>

- search ignores accents and case
- search by GTIN without leading zero and qualifier
- filter by user
- filter: only records with problems
- filter offers the qualifiers present
- filter by serial number
- filter and search together (a serial number's informative batch counts)
- search by description
- key type filter: the types present, in the editor's order, with counts
- key type filter: SSCC only; the qualifier filter follows the key type
- clear button shown while a filter is active
- qualifier filter offers the qualifiers of the GTIN records in URI order
- qualifier filter: records with a serial number (with or without other qualifiers)
- qualifier filter and text search together
- the filters are in the address (a bookmark keeps them)
- a filtered list exports only the records it shows
- qualifier filter: records created elsewhere with qualifiers the portal does not manage
- filters follow the language and keep the choice
- an incomplete code is looked up as a code
- an address that is not a Digital Link is searched as text
- search by Portuguese name
- leaving after a search by name keeps the chosen attribute
- typing a member's number finds its family
- typing filters by name
- users filtered by organisation

</details>

- search ignores accents and case

- filter offers the qualifiers present
- filter by serial number
- filter and search together (a serial number's informative batch counts)
- search by description
- key type filter: the types present, in the editor's order, with counts
- key type filter: SSCC only; the qualifier filter follows the key type
- clear button shown while a filter is active
- qualifier filter offers the qualifiers of the GTIN records in URI order
- qualifier filter: records with a serial number (with or without other qualifiers)
- qualifier filter and text search together
- the filters are in the address (a bookmark keeps them)
- a filtered list exports only the records it shows
- qualifier filter: records created elsewhere with qualifiers the portal does not manage

- an incomplete code is looked up as a code

- signed in only
- the texts in both languages

- …filtered to an organisation: its figures, as its administrators see them
- …the filter ignored for them
- the export follows the filter

<details><summary><code>admin_nav_e2e</code> — 4 checks</summary>

- …filtered by organisation
- the overview: an organisation filter and Operation for the server administrator
- …filtered: the scope named
- an organisation's administrator: no organisation filter, no Operation

<details><summary><code>exports</code> — 1 checks</summary>

- …a filtered list's export ("only": the records shown) within the limit: only those records

</details>

<details><summary><code>sso</code> — 1 checks</summary>

- …and a lapsed proof whose record the daily look finds again is no longer lapsed

</details>

<details><summary><code>api</code> — 1 checks</summary>

- list: filter by key

</details>

<details><summary><code>domains_e2e</code> — 1 checks</summary>

- …another filter changes the EPC

</details>

<details><summary><code>monitor</code> — 1 checks</summary>

- …the next verification finds it: an alert, a message to the server administrators

</details>

<details><summary><code>forwarding</code> — 1 checks</summary>

- the Requests list filters by the new outcomes

</details>

<details><summary><code>forwarding_e2e</code> — 2 checks</summary>

- Requests: the two outcomes in the filter
- …filtered, the outcome named in each row

</details>

<details><summary><code>strict_mode</code> — 1 checks</summary>

- the Requests screen filters by withheld

</details>

<details><summary><code>safety_warnings</code> — 1 checks</summary>

- fold: case, spaces, - . / removed, O and I read as 0 and 1

</details>

<details><summary><code>i18n</code> — 1 checks</summary>

- the check of a catalogue finds a missing message, other placeholders, an empty text, a link type without its explanation and an unknown message

</details>

<details><summary><code>docs</code> — 1 checks</summary>

- …and gathers every search and filter, the search by EPC included

</details>

<details><summary><code>store</code> — 4 checks</summary>

- journal: indexes for the record history and the audit filters
- audit filters: user, text, permission
- audit filters: dates inclusive
- migration: a second start finds nothing to do

</details>

<details><summary><code>least_privilege</code> — 2 checks</summary>

- db_init: the resolver's role finds records and the portal's tenants, domains, settings, branding and forwarding rules, and writes the request log (insert, update, indexes), nothing else
- the scan of the resolver's code finds the collections it is known to use

</details>

<details><summary><code>request_log</code> — 135 checks</summary>

- a redirect is answered as before
- one event per request
- method, form, key and company part
- status and outcome
- the level that answered: the key's
- the link type asked, without the vocabulary's prefix
- the data attributes' AIs, in order
- the target's host only
- the language, lower case
- the device class
- the referrer's host only
- the time taken, in whole milliseconds
- time and expiry: 90 days by default
- never the client's address
- never the User-Agent
- never the full referrer
- never the values of data attributes
- the browser family
- only the documented fields
- no country without RESOLVER_LOG_COUNTRY_HEADER
- a batch: qualifier value kept, the batch level answered
- a serial number: its AI kept, never its value (most specific level: the serial's)
- …the serial value appears nowhere in the event
- an unknown batch walks up to the key's level
- a TPX: only its AI
- an unregistered code: 404 'unknown'
- …the 404 answer is unchanged (no private keys)
- a link type the record does not have: 404 'noLink'
- …the 404 answer is unchanged

- …the 300 answer carries no private key
- a linkset: 200 'linkset', link type 'linkset'

- no Accept-Language: no language

- an automated client with a key: counted, not stored; its key, outcome, no browser

- a flag is not a number, a number is not a flag, a text is neither

- an array, a text, a number or null as the body is refused by bodies.problem

- an empty body still reaches the route, which reads it as {} (gtin.required)
- the organisations' API still creates a record from a valid body (201)
- the organisations' API still answers an empty body with request.jsonRequired (422)
- an XLSX export whose labels carry control characters is written without them (the format forbids them)

<details><summary><code>fix_serials</code> — 16 checks</summary>

- flagged: the organisation's serial numbers registered with a batch or variant
- a reader may not fix
- a key's records fixed together (its document is written back whole)
- …registered again as the serial number alone, the batch as information, description and links kept
- …the variant and batch both kept as information, in path order
- …the key's other records unchanged
- …the journal: the old record deleted, the new one created
- fix them all: a true duplicate of the serial number's own record removed; a key whose serial number has its own record with other targets left whole and reported
- …the duplicate gone, the serial number's own record kept
- …the key with a conflict unchanged (its other rule-2 record too)
- …another organisation's records untouched
- …in the journal
- another organisation's administrator fixes its own
- deleting a record of a key whose document holds one the API would refuse: deleted, that record untouched (nothing is written back since ADR 0023)
- an ordinary deletion still works
- the texts in both languages

</details>

<details><summary><code>record_delete</code> — 39 checks</summary>

- the editor deletes a batch record: 200 delete.done
- …with one call to the data entry API, the record-level delete with the batch's qualifiers
- …the key's document never deleted (no window in which its other records answer 404)
- …the key's other records kept as they were
- …in the journal, with the record as it was
- the data entry API fails the delete: 502 upstream.deleteFailed
- …every record of the key still there (the failure cannot leave the key empty)
- …nothing journalled as deleted
- …the attempt journalled first, then change-failed naming it (ADR 0026)
- a batch the key has no record of: 404 record.notFound, nothing written
- the key's own record deleted while a batch has one: the batch kept
- the last record deleted: the key's document goes with it
- a key holding a record the API would refuse: the batch deleted, that record untouched
- the tenant API deletes a batch: 200, one record-level delete, the product's record kept
- …a failed delete through the tenant API: 502, the record kept
- fixing two serial numbers: both fixed
- …the serial number written alone first, then the old record removed, one record at a time
- …the key's document never deleted
- …the records as the rule wants them, the product's kept
- the old record's removal fails: 502, the serial number answers by both records (never by none)
- …what was done is in the journal (the new record created)
- …a second run finishes it: the old record, now a repeat, removed
- writing the serial number alone fails: 502, the old record kept, nothing else written
- a fixed record the data entry API would refuse: reported (record.rewriteRefused), nothing written
- the editor's save of a new record, the key changing under other writers: 409 upstream.conflict
- …the key's records as they were, the attempt journalled as failed
- the editor's save of an existing record, the key changing under other writers: 409 upstream.conflict
- …the key's records as they were, the attempt journalled as failed
- the editor's delete, the key changing under other writers: 409 upstream.conflict
- …the key's records as they were, the attempt journalled as failed
- the tenant API's save, the key changing under other writers: 409 upstream.conflict
- …the key's records as they were, the attempt journalled as failed
- the tenant API's delete, the key changing under other writers: 409 upstream.conflict
- …the key's records as they were, the attempt journalled as failed
- fixing serial numbers while the key changes under other writers: 409 upstream.conflict, the old record kept
- the stand-in refuses a malformed record delete ({'removeRecord': False, 'qualifiers': []}): 400, nothing removed
- the stand-in refuses a malformed record delete ({'removeRecord': True, 'qualifiers': [{'10': 'L1'}): 400, nothing removed
- the stand-in refuses a malformed record delete ({'removeRecord': True, 'qualifiers': {'10': 'L1'}}): 400, nothing removed
- the stand-in refuses a malformed record delete ({'removeRecord': True, 'qualifiers': [{'10': 5}]}): 400, nothing removed

</details>

<details><summary><code>journal</code> — 29 checks</summary>

- an event written before the upgrade is sealed when the portal starts, marked so
- start-up housekeeping includes the daily checkpoint of the chains
- a record created: 201
- …its event written before the data entry API is called
- …sealed in the organisation's record chain, with the record's content
- a record updated: event first, then the API
- the journal cannot be written: 503 journal.unavailable
- …the data entry API never called, the record not created
- …an update refused the same way, the record unchanged
- …a delete refused the same way, the record kept
- …the tenant API refused the same way (503 journal.unavailable), nothing created
- the data entry API refuses a create: 502
- …change-failed names the attempt (chain and sequence number) and the message code
- …the record's history leaves the failed attempt out
- …a second, successful attempt is the history's only version
- …the audit trail shows the attempt and its failure
- an update stopped half way (the links' removal refused): 502 and change-failed upstream.partialUpdate
- a delete the API refuses: change-failed upstream.deleteFailed
- records turned to explicit default links are journalled, one update each with its new content
- verification: administrators only (an editor: 403)
- an organisation's administrator verifies the chains of its own trail, all ok, keyed
- a server administrator verifies every chain (the operator's and each organisation's)
- a receipt kept earlier: found in the chain
- a receipt of a chain outside the trail: 422 journal.badReceipt
- text that is not a receipt: 422 journal.badReceipt
- an event changed in the database: its chain reported broken at that event
- …another organisation's verification does not see it
- start-up: retention and sealing refused to the portal's user (code 13) are left to portal-db-init, and both are tried
- start-up: any other database error is not hidden

</details>
